Bangkok

The diplomatic corps in Bangkok: 168 missions, 82 resident and 86 non-resident, with attachés, consular posts and a consultants list of sitting foreign ministers. Source: Thai Ministry of Foreign Affairs, Diplomatic and Consular List, August 2026.
